- **README**: Single-responsibility agents for building features in FuzeInfra. Each agent owns **one domain**, hard-stops at its boundary, and is **forbidden from grading the whole feature** — it reports only its own slice. This exists because a monolithic feature-agent once reported "DONE/GREEN" while siblings (UI/tests) were unbuilt. Scoped agents that report an honest, scoped "done" are the guardrail.
